# The Kumulipo — Beckwith Translation & Commentary (1951)

Martha Warren Beckwith's scholarly translation and edition of the Kumulipo, *A Hawaiian Creation Chant* (University of Chicago Press, 1951). Carries the two-thousand-line Kalakaua text with section-by-section commentary, the full Hawaiian-language Kalakaua text (appendix), textual notes, and references — the comprehensive critical companion to Liliʻuokalani's earlier rendering.

Combined here from the chapter and appendix pages of the sacred-texts.com edition into one witness text, in the reading order of its index. Page markers `{p. N}` preserved; the chant-index navigation page is omitted as non-textual.

<<~/ahu >>

<<~ ahu #provenance >>

## Provenance

Source: *The Kumulipo: A Hawaiian Creation Chant*, translated and edited with commentary by Martha Warren Beckwith. University of Chicago Press [1951; copyright not renewed].

Digital edition scanned at sacred-texts.com, June 2001.

Retrieved 2026-06-21 via the Internet Archive Wayback Machine (snapshot `web.archive.org/web/20010715`) because sacred-texts.com now sits behind a Cloudflare interstitial. Source index: `https://sacred-texts.com/pac/ku/index.htm` (pages `ku00`–`ku33`; the separate `chant.htm` navigation index omitted).
